32554:   function buildTree(ds){var by={},root=[];(ds||[]).forEach(function(x){by[x.id]={id:x.id,name:x.name,code:x.code,parent:x.parent_department_id,manager:x.manager_employee_id,children:[]}});Object.keys(by).forEach(function(k){var x=by[k];if(x.parent&&by[x.parent])by[x.parent].children.push(x);else root.push(x)});function node(x,depth){var manager=H.employees.filter(function(e){return e.id===x.manager})[0];return '<div class="mr-'+Math.min(depth*3,12)+' rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-4 bg-white shadow-sm"><div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><div><div class="font-black">'+esc(x.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500">'+esc(x.code||'-')+(manager?' · مدير: '+esc(manager.name):'')+'</div></div>'+badge(x.children.length+' فرعي','info')+'</div>'+(x.children.length?'<div class="mt-3 space-y-3 border-r-2 border-slate-100 pr-4">'+x.children.map(function(c){return node(c,depth+1)}).join('')+'</div>':'')+'</div>'}return root.map(function(x){return node(x,0)}).join('')||'<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لم تُنشأ إدارات بعد</div>'}

32608:   (function installModalResilience(){
32609:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
32610:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
32611:       if(!ac)return;
32612:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
32613:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
32614:       e.preventDefault();
32615:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
32616:     },true);
32617:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
32618:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32619:       if(!root)return;
32620:       e.preventDefault();
32621:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
32622:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
32623:     });
32624:     try{
32625:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
32626:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32627:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
32628:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
32629:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
32630:       });
32631:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
32632:     }catch(e){}
32633:   }());

32489:   function opClear(k){if(k)delete H.ops[k]}
32490:   async function actor(){var a=await supabase.auth.getUser();if(a.error||!a.data.user)throw Error('جلسة المستخدم غير صالحة');var u=await supabase.from('users').select('id,email,company_id,role,name,status,phone,employee_id,default_branch_id,active_warehouse_role').eq('auth_id',a.data.user.id).maybeSingle();if(u.error)throw u.error;if(!u.data||!u.data.id||!u.data.company_id)throw Error('تعذر تحديد سياق الموظف والشركة');H.actor=u.data;H.companyId=u.data.company_id}
32491:   async function q(view,payload){var r=await supabase.rpc('hr_query',{p_view:view,p_payload:payload||{}});if(r.error)throw r.error;if(!r.data||r.data.success===false)throw Error((r.data&&(r.data.msg||r.data.code))||'فشل قراءة HR');return r.data}

32494:   function badge(text,k){var m={ok:'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-100',warn:'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-100',bad:'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border-rose-100',info:'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border-blue-100',muted:'bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 border-slate-100'};return '<span class="inline-flex items-center px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full border text-[11px] font-black '+(m[k]||m.muted)+'">'+esc(text)+'</span>'}
32495:   function card(title,sub,body,actions){return '<section class="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-100 shadow-sm overflow-hidden"><div class="px-6 py-5 bg-slate-50/80 border-b flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-center justify-between gap-3"><div><h3 class="font-black text-slate-800">'+esc(title)+'</h3><p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">'+esc(sub||'')+'</p></div><div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">'+(actions||'')+'</div></div><div class="p-6">'+body+'</div></section>'}
32496:   function stat(title,value,icon,cls){return '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-5 shadow-sm"><div class="flex items-center justify-between"><div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500 font-bold">'+esc(title)+'</div><div class="text-2xl font-black mt-2">'+esc(value)+'</div></div><div class="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center '+(cls||'bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700')+'"><i class="fas '+icon+'"></i></div></div></div>'}

32498:   function tr(cells){return '<tr class="border-t border-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-50/70">'+cells.map(function(x){return '<td class="px-4 py-3 align-top">'+x+'</td>'}).join('')+'</tr>'}

32500:   function textarea(label,id,value){return '<label class="block"><span class="block text-xs font-black text-slate-600 mb-2">'+esc(label)+'</span><textarea id="'+esc(id)+'" class="w-full px-4 py-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 min-h-[95px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-200">'+esc(value||'')+'</textarea></label>'}

32502:  function modal(title,body,onSubmit,key){

32504:   if(old)old.remove();

32508:   document.body.appendChild(r);
32509:   E('rw-hr-close').onclick=closeModal;
32510:   E('rw-hr-cancel').onclick=closeModal;
32511:   r.addEventListener('click',function(e){

32513:     if(ac){
32514:       e.preventDefault();
32515:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
32516:     }
32517:   });
32518:   if(onSubmit===null){
32519:     var f=E('rw-hr-form');
32520:     if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
32521:   }else{
32522:     E('rw-hr-form').onsubmit=async function(e){
32523:       e.preventDefault();
32524:       var save=e.target.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
32525:       try{
32526:         if(save){
32527:           save.disabled=true;
32528:           save.textContent='جارٍ الحفظ…';
32529:         }
32530:         await onSubmit(key||'form:'+Date.now());
32531:       }catch(err){
32532:         toast(err.message||'تعذر الحفظ','error');
32533:         if(save){
32534:           save.disabled=false;
32535:           save.textContent='حفظ';
32536:         }
32537:       }
32538:     };
32539:   }
32540: }
32541: function closeModal(){var r=E('rw-hr-modal-root');if(r)r.remove()}
32542:   function ppl(){return H.employees.filter(function(e){return String(e.role||'').toLowerCase()!=='owner'&&e.role!=='مالك'}).map(function(e){return{value:e.id,label:(e.name||e.email)+' — '+e.email}})}
32543:   async function loadPeople(){var d=await q('employees');H.employees=d.rows||[];return H.employees}
32544:   async function loadBranches(){var r=await supabase.from('branches').select('id,branch_code,name,is_active').eq('company_id',H.companyId).order('name');if(r.error)throw r.error;H.branches=r.data||[];return H.branches}
32545:   function branches(){return H.branches.filter(function(x){return x.is_active!==false}).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:(x.branch_code||'')+' — '+x.name}})}
32546:   function employeeOpts(){return ppl()}
32547:   function deptOpts(rows){return (rows||[]).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:x.name}})}
32548:   function posOpts(rows){return (rows||[]).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:x.title}})}
32549:   function scheduleOpts(rows){return (rows||[]).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:x.name}})}
32550:   function tabbar(){return '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 shadow-sm p-2 flex gap-2 flex-wrap">'+T.map(function(x){return '<button type="button" data-hr-tab="'+x[0]+'" class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl font-black text-sm '+(H.tab===x[0]?'bg-indigo-600 text-white':'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50')+'"><i class="fas '+x[2]+' ml-1"></i>'+x[1]+'</button>'}).join('')+'</div>'}
32551:   function employeeMeta(e){return '<div class="space-y-2 text-sm"><div><span class="text-slate-500">القسم:</span> <b>'+esc(e.department_name||e.department||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">الوظيفة:</span> <b>'+esc(e.position_name||e.job_title||e.role||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">الفرع:</span> <b>'+esc(e.branch_name||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">العقد:</span> '+(e.contract_status==='active'?badge('فعال','ok'):badge(e.contract_status||'غير موجود','muted'))+'</div></div>'}

32554:   function buildTree(ds){var by={},root=[];(ds||[]).forEach(function(x){by[x.id]={id:x.id,name:x.name,code:x.code,parent:x.parent_department_id,manager:x.manager_employee_id,children:[]}});Object.keys(by).forEach(function(k){var x=by[k];if(x.parent&&by[x.parent])by[x.parent].children.push(x);else root.push(x)});function node(x,depth){var manager=H.employees.filter(function(e){return e.id===x.manager})[0];return '<div class="mr-'+Math.min(depth*3,12)+' rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-4 bg-white shadow-sm"><div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><div><div class="font-black">'+esc(x.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500">'+esc(x.code||'-')+(manager?' · مدير: '+esc(manager.name):'')+'</div></div>'+badge(x.children.length+' فرعي','info')+'</div>'+(x.children.length?'<div class="mt-3 space-y-3 border-r-2 border-slate-100 pr-4">'+x.children.map(function(c){return node(c,depth+1)}).join('')+'</div>':'')+'</div>'}return root.map(function(x){return node(x,0)}).join('')||'<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لم تُنشأ إدارات بعد</div>'}

32567:   async function disburseAdvance(id){
32568:     var tr=await supabase.from('treasury').select('id,account_name,account_code,type,current_balance,is_active').eq('company_id',H.companyId).eq('is_active',true).in('type',['Cash','Bank']).order('account_name');
32569:     if(tr.error)throw tr.error;
32570:     var rows=tr.data||[];
32571:     if(!rows.length){toast('لا توجد خزينة أو حساب بنكي فعال للصرف','error');return;}
32572:     var opts=rows.map(function(x){return '<option value="'+esc(x.id)+'">'+esc(x.account_name||x.account_code)+' · '+esc(x.type)+' · '+money(x.current_balance)+'</option>';}).join('');
32573:     var r=await Swal.fire({title:'صرف السلفة عبر سند Payments',html:'<div class="text-right text-sm text-slate-600 mb-2">سيتم إنشاء سند صرف مركزي وقيد محاسبي وربطه بالسلفة والموظف.</div><select id="hr-advance-treasury" class="swal2-select w-full">'+opts+'</select>',showCancelButton:true,confirmButtonText:'إنشاء سند الصرف',cancelButtonText:'إلغاء',preConfirm:function(){var t=E('hr-advance-treasury');if(!t||!t.value){Swal.showValidationMessage('اختر خزينة الصرف');return false;}return t.value;}});
32574:     if(!r.isConfirmed)return;
32575:     await c('advance.disburse',{advance_id:id,treasury_id:r.value},'disburse-advance:'+id);
32576:     toast('تم الصرف من خلال سند Payments مركزي وربطه بالسلفة','success');
32577:     return render();
32578:   }

32608:   (function installModalResilience(){
32609:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
32610:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
32611:       if(!ac)return;
32612:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
32613:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
32614:       e.preventDefault();
32615:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
32616:     },true);
32617:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
32618:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32619:       if(!root)return;
32620:       e.preventDefault();
32621:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
32622:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
32623:     });
32624:     try{
32625:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
32626:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32627:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
32628:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
32629:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
32630:       });
32631:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
32632:     }catch(e){}
32633:   }());

30050: 
30051: function _exportReportCsv() {
30052:     var resultDiv = byId('report-result');
30053: 
30054:     if (!resultDiv) {
30055:         _showToast('لا يوجد تقرير للتصدير', 'info');
30056:         return;
30057:     }
30058: 
30059:     var table = resultDiv.querySelector('table');
30060: 
30061:     if (!table) {
30062:         _showToast('لا يوجد جدول قابل للتصدير', 'info');
30063:         return;
30064:     }
30065: 
30066:     var rows = table.querySelectorAll('tr');
30067:     var csv = [];
30068: 
30069:     function csvCell(value) {
30070:         var s = String(value == null ? '' : value)
30071:             .replace(/\r?\n|\r/g, ' ')
30072:             .replace(/"/g, '""');
30073: 
30074:         return '"' + s + '"';
30075:     }
30076: 
30077:     for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
30078:         var cells = rows[i].querySelectorAll('th,td');
30079:         var line = [];
30080: 
30081:         for (var j = 0; j < cells.length; j++) {
30082:             line.push(csvCell(cells[j].innerText || ''));
30083:         }
30084: 
30085:         csv.push(line.join(','));

30132:         '<style>' +
30133:         'body{font-family:Cairo,sans-serif;padding:20px;color:#111827}' +
30134:         'table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin-top:15px}' +
30135:         'th,td{border:1px solid #ddd;padding:8px}' +

30137:         '.text-center{text-align:center}' +
30138:         '</style>' +
30139:         '</head>' +
30140:         '<body>' +
30141:         resultDiv.innerHTML +
30142:         '<script>window.onload=function(){window.print();};<\/script>' +
30143:         '</body></html>';
30144: 
30145:     printWindow.document.open();
30146:     printWindow.document.write(html);
30147:     printWindow.document.close();
30148: }

32554:   function buildTree(ds){var by={},root=[];(ds||[]).forEach(function(x){by[x.id]={id:x.id,name:x.name,code:x.code,parent:x.parent_department_id,manager:x.manager_employee_id,children:[]}});Object.keys(by).forEach(function(k){var x=by[k];if(x.parent&&by[x.parent])by[x.parent].children.push(x);else root.push(x)});function node(x,depth){var manager=H.employees.filter(function(e){return e.id===x.manager})[0];return '<div class="mr-'+Math.min(depth*3,12)+' rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-4 bg-white shadow-sm"><div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><div><div class="font-black">'+esc(x.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500">'+esc(x.code||'-')+(manager?' · مدير: '+esc(manager.name):'')+'</div></div>'+badge(x.children.length+' فرعي','info')+'</div>'+(x.children.length?'<div class="mt-3 space-y-3 border-r-2 border-slate-100 pr-4">'+x.children.map(function(c){return node(c,depth+1)}).join('')+'</div>':'')+'</div>'}return root.map(function(x){return node(x,0)}).join('')||'<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لم تُنشأ إدارات بعد</div>'}

32620:       e.preventDefault();

32622:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');

32624:     try{

32630:       });
32631:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
32632:     }catch(e){}
32633:   }());

32607:   // Resilience layer: modal actions work outside the page-container, async form errors become visible, and 360 is truly read-only.
32608:   (function installModalResilience(){
32609:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
32610:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
32611:       if(!ac)return;
32612:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
32613:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
32614:       e.preventDefault();
32615:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
32616:     },true);
32617:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
32618:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32619:       if(!root)return;
32620:       e.preventDefault();
32621:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
32622:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
32623:     });
32624:     try{
32625:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
32626:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32627:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
32628:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
32629:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
32630:       });
32631:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
32632:     }catch(e){}
32633:   }());

32638: // ============================================================
32639: // RW_CRM – إدارة علاقات العملاء (CRM)
32640: // ============================================================
32641: var RW_CRM = (function() {
32642:     'use strict';
32643: 
32644:     var state = {
32645:         customers: [],
32646:         assignees: [],
32647:         kpi: {},
32648:         search: '',
32649:         activeOnly: false,
32650:         searchTimer: null
32651:     };
32652: 
32653:     function _esc(s) {
32654:         return String(s == null ? '' : s)
32655:             .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
32656:             .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
32657:             .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
32658:             .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
32659:             .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
32660:     }
32661: 
32662:     function _fmtNum(n) {
32663:         return Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('ar-EG');
32664:     }
32665: 
32666:     function _fmtMoney(n) {
32667:         return Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('ar-EG') + ' EGP';
32668:     }
32669: 
32670:     function _today() {
32671:         var d = new Date();
32672:         var local = new Date(d.getTime() - d.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000);
32673:         return local.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
32674:     }
32675: 
32676:     function _statusLabel(s) {
32677:         var map = {
32678:             Open: 'مفتوحة',
32679:             'معلقة': 'معلقة',
32680:             completed: 'مكتملة',
32681:             'مكتملة': 'مكتملة',
32682:             cancelled: 'ملغاة',
32683:             'ملغاة': 'ملغاة'
32684:         };
32685:         return map[s] || s || 'غير محددة';
32686:     }
32687: 
32688:     function _statusClass(s) {
32689:         if (s === 'completed' || s === 'مكتملة') return 'bg-green-100 text-green-700';
32690:         if (s === 'cancelled' || s === 'ملغاة') return 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600';
32691:         return 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700';
32692:     }
32693: 
32694:     function _assignedLabel(customer) {
32695:         var rows = Array.isArray(customer && customer.assigned_to) ? customer.assigned_to : [];
32696:         if (!rows.length) return 'غير مسند';
32697:         var active = rows.filter(function(x) { return x && x.active !== false; });
32698:         if (!active.length) active = rows;
32699:         return active.slice(0, 2).map(function(x) {
32700:             return x.name || x.email || '—';
32701:         }).join('، ') + (active.length > 2 ? ' +' + (active.length - 2) : '');
32702:     }

32704:     async function _loadDirectory() {
32705:         var res = await supabase.rpc('crm_customer_directory', {
32706:             p_search: state.search || null,
32707:             p_active_only: state.activeOnly,
32708:             p_limit: 200,
32709:             p_offset: 0
32710:         });
32711:         if (res.error) throw res.error;
32712: 
32713:         var payload = res.data || {};
32714:         state.customers = Array.isArray(payload.customers) ? payload.customers : [];
32715:         state.assignees = Array.isArray(payload.assignees) ? payload.assignees : [];
32716:         state.kpi = payload.kpi || {};
32717:         return payload;
32718:     }
32719: 
32720:     function _renderKpis() {
32721:         var k = state.kpi || {};
32722:         return '<div class="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-6 gap-4">' +
32723:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">إجمالي العملاء</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-indigo-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.total_customers) + '</div></div>' +
32724:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">عملاء نشطون</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-green-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.active_customers) + '</div></div>' +
32725:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">ذمم مسجلة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-red-600 mt-2">' + _fmtMoney(k.master_debt_total) + '</div></div>' +
32726:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">متابعات مفتوحة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-amber-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.open_followups) + '</div></div>' +
32727:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">متأخرة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-rose-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.overdue_followups) + '</div></div>' +
32728:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">مستحقة اليوم</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-blue-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.due_today) + '</div></div>' +
32729:         '</div>';
32730:     }
32731: 
32732:     function _renderTable() {
32733:         if (!state.customers.length) {
32734:             return '<div class="text-center py-16 text-gray-400"><div class="text-5xl mb-3">👥</div><div class="font-black text-lg">لا توجد عملاء مطابقون</div><div class="text-sm mt-2">غيّر البحث أو الفلاتر ثم أعد المحاولة.</div></div>';
32735:         }
32736: 
32737:         var html = '<div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-sm">' +
32738:             '<thead class="bg-slate-50"><tr>' +
32739:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">العميل</th>' +
32740:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">التواصل</th>' +
32741:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">التصنيف</th>' +
32742:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">المبيعات</th>' +
32743:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">الأوردرات</th>' +
32744:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">المتابعة القادمة</th>' +
32745:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">المسؤول</th>' +
32746:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">الإجراء</th>' +
32747:             '</tr></thead><tbody>';
32748: 
32749:         for (var i = 0; i < state.customers.length; i++) {
32750:             var c = state.customers[i] || {};
32751:             var overdue = Number(c.overdue_followups || 0) > 0;
32752:             var next = c.next_followup_date ? String(c.next_followup_date) : '—';
32753: 
32754:             html += '<tr class="border-b hover:bg-slate-50">' +
32755:                 '<td class="p-3"><div class="font-black">' + _esc(c.name) + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.customer_code) + '</div></td>' +
32756:                 '<td class="p-3"><div>' + _esc(c.phone || '—') + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.area || '—') + '</div></td>' +
32757:                 '<td class="p-3"><div class="font-bold">' + _esc(c.customer_type || '—') + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.payment_type || '—') + '</div></td>' +
32758:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center font-black">' + _fmtMoney(c.sales_total) + '</td>' +
32759:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center font-black">' + _fmtNum(c.order_count) + '</td>' +
32760:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center"><span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-black ' + (overdue ? 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700') + '">' + _esc(next) + '</span></td>' +
32761:                 '<td class="p-3">' + _esc(_assignedLabel(c)) + '</td>' +
32762:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center"><button type="button" data-crm-open360="' + _esc(c.id) + '" class="px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-xl font-black">متابعة</button></td>' +
32763:             '</tr>';
32764:         }
32765: 
32766:         return html + '</tbody></table></div>';
32767:     }
32768: 
32769:     function _bindDirectory() {
32770:         var search = byId('crm-search');
32771:         if (search) {
32772:             search.value = state.search;
32773:             search.addEventListener('input', function() {
32774:                 state.search = search.value.trim();
32775:                 clearTimeout(state.searchTimer);
32776:                 state.searchTimer = setTimeout(function() {
32777:                     _loadDirectory().then(function() {
32778:                         safeHTML(byId('crm-customers-list'), _renderTable());
32779:                         _bindOpen360();
32780:                         safeHTML(byId('crm-kpis'), _renderKpis());
32781:                     }).catch(function(e) {
32782:                         showToast(e.message || 'فشل البحث', 'error');
32783:                     });
32784:                 }, 250);
32785:             });
32786:         }

32482:   function num(v){v=Number(v);return isFinite(v)?v:0}

32490:   async function actor(){var a=await supabase.auth.getUser();if(a.error||!a.data.user)throw Error('جلسة المستخدم غير صالحة');var u=await supabase.from('users').select('id,email,company_id,role,name,status,phone,employee_id,default_branch_id,active_warehouse_role').eq('auth_id',a.data.user.id).maybeSingle();if(u.error)throw u.error;if(!u.data||!u.data.id||!u.data.company_id)throw Error('تعذر تحديد سياق الموظف والشركة');H.actor=u.data;H.companyId=u.data.company_id}

32553:   async function employeesTab(cn){await loadPeople();var rows=H.employees.filter(function(e){return String(e.role||'').toLowerCase()!=='owner'&&e.role!=='مالك'});cn.innerHTML=card('دليل الموظفين','Employee 360 من مركز واحد','<div class="flex gap-2 mb-5"><input id="hr-emp-search" class="flex-1 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl border" placeholder="بحث بالاسم أو البريد أو الرقم أو الوظيفة">'+btn('ملف موظف','new-profile')+'</div><div id="hr-emp-grid" class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-4">'+rows.map(function(e){var total=num(e.basic_salary)+num(e.housing_allowance)+num(e.transport_allowance)+num(e.other_allowance)-num(e.default_deduction);return '<article data-eid="'+esc(e.id)+'" class="p-5 bg-white border border-slate-100 rounded-2xl cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md"><div class="flex items-center gap-3"><div class="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-indigo-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center text-xl font-black">'+esc((e.name||'?')[0])+'</div><div class="min-w-0"><div class="font-black truncate">'+esc(e.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500 truncate">'+esc(e.position_name||e.job_title||e.role||'-')+'</div></div></div><div class="mt-4">'+employeeMeta(e)+'</div><div class="mt-4 pt-3 border-t flex justify-between text-sm"><span class="text-slate-500">التعويض الحالي</span><b class="text-indigo-700">'+money(total)+' EGP</b></div></article>'}).join('')+'</div>');var s=E('hr-emp-search');if(s)s.oninput=function(){var v=s.value.toLowerCase();cn.querySelectorAll('[data-eid]').forEach(function(el){var e=rows.filter(function(x){return x.id===el.getAttribute('data-eid')})[0]||{};var h=[e.name,e.email,e.employee_number,e.job_title,e.department_name,e.position_name].join(' ').toLowerCase();el.style.display=!v||h.indexOf(v)>-1?'':'none'})};cn.querySelectorAll('[data-eid]').forEach(function(el){el.onclick=function(){open360(el.getAttribute('data-eid'))}})}
32554:   function buildTree(ds){var by={},root=[];(ds||[]).forEach(function(x){by[x.id]={id:x.id,name:x.name,code:x.code,parent:x.parent_department_id,manager:x.manager_employee_id,children:[]}});Object.keys(by).forEach(function(k){var x=by[k];if(x.parent&&by[x.parent])by[x.parent].children.push(x);else root.push(x)});function node(x,depth){var manager=H.employees.filter(function(e){return e.id===x.manager})[0];return '<div class="mr-'+Math.min(depth*3,12)+' rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-4 bg-white shadow-sm"><div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><div><div class="font-black">'+esc(x.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500">'+esc(x.code||'-')+(manager?' · مدير: '+esc(manager.name):'')+'</div></div>'+badge(x.children.length+' فرعي','info')+'</div>'+(x.children.length?'<div class="mt-3 space-y-3 border-r-2 border-slate-100 pr-4">'+x.children.map(function(c){return node(c,depth+1)}).join('')+'</div>':'')+'</div>'}return root.map(function(x){return node(x,0)}).join('')||'<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لم تُنشأ إدارات بعد</div>'}

32562:  var financial=d.financial_balances||[],debt=0,payable=0;

32564:  var financialBody='<div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-5">'+stat('إجمالي المديونية على الموظفين',money(debt)+' ج.م','fa-file-invoice-dollar','bg-amber-50 text-amber-700')+stat('مستحقات الموظفين لدى الشركة',money(payable)+' ج.م','fa-hand-holding-usd','bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700')+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500 mb-4">الأرصدة من القيود المرحلة فقط. الموجب على الموظف، والسالب مستحق له. طلبات السلف غير المصروفة لا تدخل في هذه الأرقام.</div>'+table(['الموظف','الحالة','طبيعة الذمة','الحساب','إجمالي المدين','إجمالي الدائن','الرصيد واتجاهه'],financial.map(function(x){var b=num(x.outstanding_balance),dir=b>0?badge('على الموظف','warn'):b<0?badge('مستحق للموظف','ok'):badge('متزن','muted');return tr([esc(x.employee_name||'-'),esc(x.employee_status||'-'),esc(types[x.party_nature]||x.party_nature||'-'),esc((x.account_code||'')+' — '+(x.account_name||'')),money(x.total_debit)+' ج.م',money(x.total_credit)+' ج.م',dir+' <b class="mr-2">'+money(Math.abs(b))+' ج.م</b>'])}));

32566: }
32567:   async function disburseAdvance(id){
32568:     var tr=await supabase.from('treasury').select('id,account_name,account_code,type,current_balance,is_active').eq('company_id',H.companyId).eq('is_active',true).in('type',['Cash','Bank']).order('account_name');
32569:     if(tr.error)throw tr.error;
32570:     var rows=tr.data||[];
32571:     if(!rows.length){toast('لا توجد خزينة أو حساب بنكي فعال للصرف','error');return;}
32572:     var opts=rows.map(function(x){return '<option value="'+esc(x.id)+'">'+esc(x.account_name||x.account_code)+' · '+esc(x.type)+' · '+money(x.current_balance)+'</option>';}).join('');
32573:     var r=await Swal.fire({title:'صرف السلفة عبر سند Payments',html:'<div class="text-right text-sm text-slate-600 mb-2">سيتم إنشاء سند صرف مركزي وقيد محاسبي وربطه بالسلفة والموظف.</div><select id="hr-advance-treasury" class="swal2-select w-full">'+opts+'</select>',showCancelButton:true,confirmButtonText:'إنشاء سند الصرف',cancelButtonText:'إلغاء',preConfirm:function(){var t=E('hr-advance-treasury');if(!t||!t.value){Swal.showValidationMessage('اختر خزينة الصرف');return false;}return t.value;}});
32574:     if(!r.isConfirmed)return;
32575:     await c('advance.disburse',{advance_id:id,treasury_id:r.value},'disburse-advance:'+id);
32576:     toast('تم الصرف من خلال سند Payments مركزي وربطه بالسلفة','success');
32577:     return render();
32578:   }

32606:   function realtime(){try{if(H.channel)supabase.removeChannel(H.channel);var refresh=function(){clearTimeout(H.timer);H.timer=setTimeout(function(){render()},700)};var tables=['employee_profiles','employee_attendance','employee_leave_requests','employee_documents','hr_departments','hr_positions','hr_employee_assignments','hr_employee_schedule_assignments','hr_work_schedules','hr_attendance_events','hr_work_entries','hr_leave_types','hr_leave_balances','hr_requests','hr_request_approvals','hr_salary_advances','hr_salary_components','hr_contracts','hr_contract_components','hr_payroll_periods','hr_payroll_runs','hr_payslips','hr_payslip_lines','hr_payroll_accounting_map'];H.channel=supabase.channel('rw-hr-mother-final');tables.forEach(function(t){H.channel.on('postgres_changes',{event:'*',schema:'public',table:t},refresh)});H.channel.on('postgres_changes',{event:'*',schema:'public',table:'journal_lines',filter:'party_type=eq.Employee'},refresh);H.channel.on('postgres_changes',{event:'*',schema:'public',table:'cash_box',filter:'party_type=eq.Employee'},refresh);H.channel.on('postgres_changes',{event:'UPDATE',schema:'public',table:'journal_entries',filter:'status=eq.Posted'},refresh);H.channel.subscribe()}catch(e){console.warn('RW_HR realtime',e)}}
32607:   // Resilience layer: modal actions work outside the page-container, async form errors become visible, and 360 is truly read-only.
32608:   (function installModalResilience(){
32609:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
32610:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
32611:       if(!ac)return;
32612:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
32613:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
32614:       e.preventDefault();
32615:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
32616:     },true);
32617:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
32618:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32619:       if(!root)return;
32620:       e.preventDefault();
32621:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
32622:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
32623:     });
32624:     try{
32625:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
32626:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32627:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
32628:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
32629:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
32630:       });
32631:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
32632:     }catch(e){}
32633:   }());

32635: realtime(); return { render: render, reload: render, openEmployee360: open360 }; }()); window.RW_HR = RW_HR;
